Theorem 218.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let ρ be a convergence analysis functional on the hyperbolic space X. Then the minimizer x* satisfies ||x*|| ≤ 969 and the convergence rate is O(506/n^3).

Proof: We construct the required map explicitly. Starting from the convergence analysis condition on hyperbolic structure S, we define the operator T by T(x) = Σ c_k x^k where |c_k| ≤ 969. The convergence follows from the convergence analysis estimate ||T^n|| ≤ 506. QED.
